The owner clarified the full same-checkpoint, similar-quality performance goal and authorized focused two-bit, lower-bit VQ, mixed-format and runtime experiments, while retaining the ban on the huge statistical campaign. The active plan now records the missed lower budgets, the original's successful tested Desktop ceiling, explicit experiment hypotheses/advance/stop conditions, and both independent automatic systems. This scope is not limited to shipping three-bit.

The already frozen prefill/cache mechanism comparison failed quiet-host admission twice. The first attempt launched no model; the second completed only the automatic cell and stopped before launching the capped cell. No paired benefit is computed or selected from that incomplete prefix. Both Photos guards record successful restoration. All logs, frozen inputs and build checks are preserved. Twenty-four Python runner checks and eighty-eight native protocol assertions pass. This is a deferred mechanism comparison, not evidence of a failed performance hypothesis.

A separately frozen, task-local component screen authenticates original files, reads ten declared experts from each of three declared layers, and compares original four-bit, requantized three-bit, requantized two-bit and two projection mixtures. Its input activations are deterministic synthetic probes, not captured task activations. It uses the installed MLX 0.32.2 gathered kernels with one and three token rows, five alternating-order rounds and forty iterations per recipe. The packed RHS bank contains ten selected experts; this is not a full production pool/cache or application benchmark. No weights or raw vocabulary arrays are saved and no public format is implemented.

All three layers complete in 29.286 seconds with lifetime physical peak 618,316,592 bytes. All observed thermal states are nominal, low-power mode is off, and whole-host swap-ins/out are unchanged. One isolated renderer CPU observation exceeds fifty percent; the frozen three-consecutive-sample exclusion does not fire. The source preserves every timing and CPU sample. Numerical results are functional observations independent of clean-timing eligibility.

Keeping the down projection at three bits while reducing gate/up to two bits is the only useful lower-bit lead in this small screen. Its three-row median kernel times are 0.379/0.366/0.370 ms, versus 0.396/0.394/0.392 ms for all-three-bit. All-two-bit and the two-bit-down mixture are slower on those verification rows. Two-bit weight reconstruction error is about three and a half times the three-bit error; the nonlinear output error also grows for the promising mixture. These are reconstruction probes, not task quality or a twenty-token result. The next cheap test is bounded full-model distribution quality with original non-expert components and transient conversion, before exporting another pack. Preserve the original and do not promote any recipe from this screen.

Exact small files follow with original bytes and SHA-256, zlib/base64 encoded. No model weights, secrets or raw logits are embedded.
